The output is CSV on standard output with\nthe header `column,type,rows,nulls,distinct,min,max,sum,mean,median`, followed by one row per\ncolumn in file order. Line endings are `\\n`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command reads the file, trims, rejects malformed input, infers each column's type, and\n  computes the counts and statistics defined by the stories it consumes.\n- The output is built in memory and written to standard output only after every column has been\n  computed, so a failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n- Output uses Go's `encoding/csv` writer with `UseCRLF` off, so a field containing a comma, a\n  double quote, or a newline is quoted as RFC 4180 specifies.\n- A header-only file produces one row per header column with type `empty`, `rows` 0, and every\n  other field zero or blank.\n- Integer `min`, `max`, and `sum` are plain integers. Number `min`, `max`, and `sum`, and every\n  `mean` and `median`, carry exactly two decimals produced by `profile.FormatDecimal`.\n- Exit status is 0 on success; a rejected file exits 3 with empty standard output.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC profile-output-columns ===\nIntent: The profile has the declared header and one row per column in file order, with types, counts, integer sum, and decimal sum and mean derived from the input.\nRequires: executable=go; quote is quoted.\n- Rejected input (ragged, empty, bad header) exits 3 with empty standard output.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC freq-count-ordering ===\nIntent: Values are listed by count lineterminator=\"\\n\").writerows(rows)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"v\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, code\nparsed = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert ARTIFACT FEATURE-UsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: Usage Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Reject malformed command-line arguments with exit status 2 and a single usage line on standard error. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Arguments.md |\n| Provides    | exit code 2 |\n| Consumes    | cli.ParseArgs |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMalformed invocations exit 2, write nothing to standard output, and write one diagnostic line to\nstandard error that shows the two command forms:\n\n```text\ncsvstat [--column <name>] <file>\ncsvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>\n```\n\n## Behavior\n\nExit 2 for each of:\n\n- no file argument\n- more than one file argument\n- an unknown option\n- an option given without its value\n- `--top` that is not a positive integer (zero, negative, or non-numeric)\n- `--top` without `--freq`\n- `--column` together with `--freq`\n- a repeated option\n\nUsage errors are decided from the arguments alone, before the file is opened, and exit 2 even when\nthe file exists and is valid.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-errors-exit-two ===\nIntent: Every malformed argument form exits 2 and writes nothing to standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nrows = [[\"qty\", \"sensor\"], [\"1\", \"east\"], [\"2\", \"west\"]]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"in.csv\")\n    other = os.path.join(d, \"other.csv\")\n    for p in (path, other):\n        with open(p, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            csv.writer(f, lineterminator=\"\\n\").writerows(rows)\n    cases = [\n        [],\n        [path, other],\n        [\"--frobnicate\", path],\n        [\"--column\"],\n        [\"--freq\"],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\"],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\", \"0\", path],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\", \"-1\", path],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\", \"abc\", path],\n        [\"--top\", \"3\", path],\n        [\"--column\", \"qty\", \"--freq\", \"qty\", path],\n    ]\n    for args in cases:\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n        print(args, code)\n        print(err)\n        assert code == 2, (args, code)\n        assert out == \"\", (args, out)\n=== END AC usage-errors-exit-two ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-single-stderr-line ===\nIntent: A usage error writes exactly one non-blank diagnostic line to standard error.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner()\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2, code\nassert out == \"\"\nlines = [ln for ln in err.splitlines() if ln.strip()]\nassert len(lines) == 1, lines\n=== END AC usage-errors-single-stderr-line ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-before-file-open ===\nIntent: A usage error is reported as 2 even when the named file does not exist.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    missing = os.path.join(d, usage-errors-valid-forms-accepted ===\nIntent: Well-formed invocations are not rejected as usage errors.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nrows = [[\"qty\", \"sensor\"], [\"1\", \"east\"], [\"2\", \"west\"]]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"in.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        csv.writer(f, lineterminator=\"\\n\").writerows(rows)\n    for args in ([path], [\"--column\", \"qty\", path], [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", path],\n | csvstat <file> |\n| Consumes    | csvdata.Read, profile.InferType, profile.Counts, profile.FormatDecimal, cli.ParseArgs |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`csvstat <file>` profiles every Line endings are `\\n`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command reads the file, trims, rejects malformed input, infers each column's type, and\n  computes the counts and statistics defined by the stories it consumes.\n- The output is built in memory and written to standard output only after every column has been\n  computed, so a failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n- Output uses Go's `encoding/csv` writer with `UseCRLF` off, so a field containing a comma, a\n  double quote, or a newline is quoted as RFC 4180 specifies.\n- A header-only file produces one row per header column with type `empty`, `rows` 0, and every\n  other field zero or blank.\n- Integer `min`, `max`, and `sum` are plain integers. ARTIFACT FEATURE-UsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: Usage Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Reject malformed command-line arguments with exit status 2 and a single usage line on standard error. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Arguments.md |\n| Provides    | exit code 2 |\n| Consumes    | cli.ParseArgs |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMalformed invocations exit 2, write nothing to standard output, and write one diagnostic line to\nstandard error that shows the two command forms:\n\n```text\ncsvstat [--column <name>] <file>\ncsvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>\n```\n\n## Behavior\n\nExit 2 for each of:\n\n- no file argument\n- more than one file argument\n- an unknown option\n- an option given without its value\n- `--top` that is not a positive integer (zero, negative, or non-numeric)\n- `--top` without `--freq`\n- `--column` together with `--freq`\n- a repeated option\n\nUsage errors are decided from the arguments alone, before the file is opened, and exit 2 even when\nthe file exists and is valid.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-errors-exit-two ===\nIntent: Every malformed argument form exits 2 and writes nothing to standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nrows = [[\"qty\", \"sensor\"], [\"1\", \"east\"], [\"2\", \"west\"]]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"in.csv\")\n    other = os.path.join(d, \"other.csv\")\n    for p in (path, other):\n        with open(p, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            csv.writer(f, lineterminator=\"\\n\").writerows(rows)\n    cases = [\n        [],\n        [path, other],\n        [\"--frobnicate\", path],\n        [\"--column\"],\n        [\"--freq\"],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\"],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\", \"0\", path],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\", \"-1\", path],\n        [\"--freq\", \"sensor\", \"--top\", \"abc\", path],\n        [\"--top\", \"3\", path],\n        [\"--column\", \"qty\", \"--freq\", \"qty\", path],\n    ]\n    for args in cases:\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n        print(args, code)\n        print(err)\n        assert code == 2, (args, code)\n        assert out == \"\", (args, out)\n=== END AC usage-errors-exit-two ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-single-stderr-line ===\nIntent: A usage error writes exactly one non-blank diagnostic line to standard error.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner()\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2, code\nassert out == \"\"\nlines = [ln for ln in err.splitlines() if ln.strip()]\nassert len(lines) == 1, lines\n=== END AC usage-errors-single-stderr-line ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-before-file-open ===\nIntent: A usage error is reported as 2 even when the named file does not exist.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    missing = os.path.join(d, import cli_runner\n\nrows = [[\"qty\", \"sensor\"], [\"1\", \"east\"], [\"2\", \"west\"]]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"in.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\",
